Implementation plan in detail:

Functions:
- `_parent_array`: get height_sequence_1 as float, clip, ensure finite sum>0.
- `_normalize(p)`: p = np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12 return ones/n; else p/s.
- `_conv_array(a)`: Use `np.convolve(a,a,method='auto')`? Numpy has method? `np.convolve` doesn't have method. Use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve` if scipy.signal imported. But to match evaluator and avoid import, use `np.convolve` for n below maybe 2048; for larger use FFT. Simpler: implement `_conv_fft` with rfft for all, but for n=384 ok. Need avoid tiny negative from FFT; maximum(c,0,out=c). For n small, direct `np.convolve` exact and faster. Could decide: if n<=1024 use `np.convolve`, else FFT. `np.convolve` returns full. For coordinate update, maintain c from `np.convolve` initially.
- `_score_from_c(c, S, n)`: if S<1e-12 inf; M=max(c); return 2*n*M/(S*S). Ensure c nonnegative.
- `_exact_score(p)`: use evaluate_sequence; but for internal avoid list conversion? We can trust p. Final track uses exact.

Root implementation details:
```
def _root_cap(c, T, min_ratio=1e-12):
    L=len(c); n=L//2
    ct=np.minimum(c, T)
    # force symmetry: ct[0] and ct[-1]? c symmetric; np.minimum preserves.
    # add tiny floor to ct[0] to avoid zero?
    tol = max(1e-30, 1e-12*T)
    d=0
    if ct[0]<=tol:
       # find first significant index r
       sig = np.where(ct > tol)[0]
       if len(sig):
          r=sig[0]
          if r %2 ==0 and ct[:r].max()<=tol:
             d=r//2
          else:
             d=0; ct[0]=tol  # force d0
       else:
          return None
    a=np.zeros(n)
    if d==0:
       a0 = math.sqrt(max(ct[0], tol))
       a[0]=a0
       for k in range(1,n):
          pair = 0.0
          if k>1:
             pair = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
          val=(ct[k]-pair)/(2*a0)
          if val < 0:
             # if small negative clip; track
             if val > -1e-9*max(1,T): val=0
             else: neg_count++
          a[k]=max(0,val)
    else:
       b_len=n-d
       D=ct[2*d:2*d+b_len]  # length b_len? ct length L, 2d+b_len =2d+n-d = n+d <= L+? max 2n-2? n+d≤2n-2 iff d≤n-2; ok if d≤n-2. If d=n-1, b_len1 D last? 2d=2n-2 out? only one coeff.
       if len(D)<b_len: D=np.concatenate([D, zeros])
       b0=sqrt(max(D[0],tol)); b=zeros(b_len); b[0]=b0
       for k in range(1,b_len):
           pair=dot(b[1:k], b[k-1:0:-1])
           val=(D[k]-pair)/(2*b0)
           clip
       a[d:]=b
    # If a sum <=0 return None
    # Optional: enforce nonnegative via clip; maybe if neg ratio >0.05 reject? Could try both clip and reject.
    return a
```
But exact root of ct with d=0 uses ct[0..n-1]. If ct[0] was forced tol but actual c0=0, high coefficients may force negative.
```
def _root_improve(p, stop, seed, max_rounds=20):
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    # p normalized
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=_score_conv(conv(p),1,n)
    c=conv(best)
    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
        M = float(c.max())
        # targets: include current M*0.999 down to maybe 0.3*M, plus average* ratios
        targets = []
        for q in [0.999,0.99,0.98,0.97,0.95,0.93,0.9,0.87,0.84,0.8,0.75,0.7,0.65,0.6,0.55,0.5,0.45,0.4,0.3]:
            targets.append(q*M)
        # also percentile targets
        for pct in [50,75,90,95,99]:
            targets.append(float(np.percentile(c,pct)))
        # maybe score target abs = (target_score/(2n)) * 1? Since normalized sum1. Add target_score 1.5? T_target=1.5027/(2n)
        targets.append(1.5027/(2*n)) # desired raw max for sum1
        targets = unique positive sorted descending? We want decreasing from M to lower. We'll evaluate all; root with T > M no cap (reconstruction). Use only <=M*(1+1e-9)
        best_T_candidate=None
        # For each T, root, normalize, score, mix?
        for T in targets (maybe 30):
             if time > stop-2: break
             a = _root_cap(c, T, n)
             if a None: continue
             # maybe try mixtures with best
             na=a/sum
             sc=_score_conv(conv(na),1,n)
             if sc<best_sc: update
             for lam in [0.2,0.5,0.8]:
                m=normalize((1-lam)*best + lam*na)
                sc2=_score...
                if sc2<best_sc update
        if best_sc improved: c=conv(best); continue else break
    return best
```

```
    p normalized; c=conv; S=1; best=p,sc.
    rng
    it=0
    while time<stop:
       if it%200==0: M=c.max(); active = np.where(c >= M - 0.05*M)[0]
       if active.size and rng.random()<0.7:
          k = active[int(rng.integers(active.size))]
          # valid i range: max(0,k-n+1)..min(k,n-1)

          i = int(rng.integers(lo,hi+1)); j=k-i

          i,j random distinct

```

```
def _root_candidates(c, T, n):
    ct = np.minimum(c, T).copy()
    # ensure symmetry? ct = 0.5*(ct + ct[::-1])? Since c symmetric, min symmetric but floating maybe. symmetrize to avoid root high mismatch:
    ct = 0.5*(ct + ct[::-1])
    tol = max(1e-30, 1e-12*max(T, c.max()))
    roots=[]
    # d=0 forced:
    ct0 = ct.copy(); ct0[0] = max(ct0[0], tol); # if ct0[0] small but not zero, maybe using tol too big? Use max(ct0[0], min(tol, 1e-6*T?))
    a = _root_from_d0(ct0,n); if finite roots.append(a)
    # delayed if c has zeros:
    # find smallest r such that ct[r] > 10*tol and all ct[:r]<=10*tol, r even
    r=0
    # because ct symmetric, low end maybe all small;
    while r < n and ct[r] <= tol: r+=1
    if r>0 and r%2==0:
       d=r//2
       if d < n:
         a=_root_delayed(ct,d,n,tol); roots.append(a)
    # Also try d=0 with original ct0 (if c0>tol)
    return roots
```
But if ct[0] small (e.g. 1e-12) and tol=1e-12*T, forcing to tol may alter. Use `eps0 = max(ct[0], 1e-18*T)`; if T ~0.002, eps0~2e-21. Good.
Root d0:
```
def _root_from_d0(ct,n):
    a=np.zeros(n)
    a0=math.sqrt(max(ct[0],0))
    if a0 <= 1e-18: return None
    a[0]=a0
    neg_abs=0.0; neg_cnt=0; maxval=0.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        if k>1:
            # np.dot length k-1
            pair = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
        else: pair=0.0
        val = (ct[k] - pair) / (2*a0)
        maxval=max(maxval, abs(val))
        if val < 0:
            neg_abs += -val
            if -val > 1e-8*max(1.0, max(abs(pair),abs(ct[k]))): neg_cnt+=1
        a[k] = max(0.0, val)
    # reject if many large negatives? Instead of reject, return with quality ratio
    quality = neg_abs / max(1e-30, np.sum(ct[:n]))
    if quality > 0.25: # too invalid
       # maybe try with clipped ct? reject
       return None
    return a, quality
```
Return quality to prefer lower. Delayed similarly.

```
D=ct[2*d:2*d+b_len]
b0=sqrt(D[0]); for k in range(1,b_len): pair=dot(b[1:k],b[k-1:0:-1]); val=(D[k]-pair)/(2*b0)
```

```
def _gradient_soft(p, stop, seed, betas=(50,150,400), max_iter_total=5000):
  p normalized; c=conv; best...
  for beta in betas:
    iters = max(200, max_iter_total//len)
    for it in range(iters):
       if time>stop break
       c=np.convolve(p,p); M=c.max(); S=1 (keep normalized; after projection sum1)
       if best improve? 
       z=beta*(c-M)
       # to avoid all zero if beta large, add small: w=exp(z); if sum==0, active top.
       w=np.exp(z); sw=w.sum(); if sw<1: active=np.argsort(c)[-5:]; w[active]=1; w/=w.sum()
       else w/=sw
       grad = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, p, mode='valid')  # length n? Check indices: np.correlate(w,p,'valid') returns index i: Σ_{j=0}^{len(p)-1} w[i+j] p[j]? Need verify numpy correlate convention. Let's test mentally: `np.correlate([a,b,c],[x,y],'valid')` length1: a*b + c*y? For full, correlate[a,v] c_k = ∑_n a[n+k] conjugate(v[n]) for k=0..M-N. So for 'valid', c[i]=∑_{j=0}^{N-1} w[i+j] p[j]. Yes correct.
       gmax = max(abs(grad), 1e-30)
       # step candidates: base = p.mean() (1/n) * maybe 0.1? Use step = (0.1,0.3,1,3,10,30) * p.mean()/gmax
       best_step=None
       for coeff in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8]:
           step = coeff * max(p.mean(),1e-12)/gmax
           q = p - step*grad
           q = _proj_simplex(q)
           sc = _score_conv(np.convolve(q,q),1,n)
           if best ...
       if best_step: p=q; c=...; update best
       else: break
  return best
```
Projection onto simplex:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(v)+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```

```
def _lp_direction(f,M):
    n=len(f); L=2n-1
    A = np.empty((L,n), dtype=float)  # initialize 0
    # A[i:i+n, i] = f
    for i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i] = f
    b = np.full(L, M)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options=dict(presolve=True))
    if status!=0: return None
    g=res.x; s=g.sum(); if s<=1+1e-12? return maybe g/s else None
    g/=s
    return g
```
But constraint f*g≤M. If f sum1 and current M. Is f feasible? f*f≤M yes. If due numerical M maybe less than max, add margin: M*=1+1e-10. LP objective maximize sum. Since f feasible with sum1, optimum sum≥1. If g same f (dist small), no direction. Line search.

We can also normalize LP with `b_ub = M * (1+1e-9)` to avoid infeasible due roundoff.

```
def _search_transfer(i,j,p,c,L,n):
    ks1=np.arange(i,i+n); ks2=np.arange(j,j+n)
    ks = np.unique(np.concatenate((ks1,ks2)))
    mask = np.zeros(L,dtype=bool); mask[ks]=True
    non_max = float(np.max(c[~mask])) if (~mask).any() else 0.0
    vi = (ks >= i) & (ks < i+n)
    vj = (ks >= j) & (ks < j+n)
    pi = np.where(vi, p[np.clip(ks-i,0,n-1)], 0.0)
    pj = np.where(vj, p[np.clip(ks-j,0,n-1)], 0.0)
    Acoef = pi - pj
    G = np.zeros(len(ks))
    pos2i = np.searchsorted(ks, 2*i)
    if pos2i < len(ks) and ks[pos2i] == 2*i:
        G[pos2i] += 1.0
    pos2j = np.searchsorted(ks, 2*j)
    if pos2j < len(ks) and ks[pos2j] == 2*j:
        G[pos2j] += 1.0
    ksum = i+j
    poscross = np.searchsorted(ks, ksum)
    if poscross < len(ks) and ks[poscross] == ksum:
        G[poscross] -= 2.0
    t_list = ...
    best_t=0; best_sc=inf; best_delta=None
    for t in t_list:
       delta = t*Acoef + G*(t*t)
       m_aff = np.max(c[ks] + delta)
       M = m_aff if m_aff > non_max else non_max
       if M < 0: M=0
       sc = 2*n*M  # S=1
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
          best_sc=sc; best_t=t; best_delta=delta
    # optional golden? If best inner, do small refinement. But candidate fractions enough.
    return best_t,best_sc,best_delta,ks
```

```
   n=len(a)

       return np.maximum(np.convolve(a,a),0.0)
   L=2*n-1
   N=1
   while N < L: N <<=1
```

```
def _root_candidates(c,T,n):
    L=2*n-1
    ct=np.minimum(c.astype(float), T)
    ct=0.5*(ct+ct[::-1])
    maxT = max(float(T), 1e-300)
    tol=1e-14*maxT + 1e-30
    outs=[]
    # d=0 forced with small eps
    ct0=ct.copy()
    if ct0[0] < 1e-18*maxT: ct0[0]=max(1e-18*maxT, np.finfo(float).tiny)
    a=_root_d(ct0,0,n,tol)
    if a is not None: outs.append(a)
    # delayed
    # find first r where ct[r] > 10*tol and all previous <=10*tol? Actually if ct[0] small but ct[1] not even, no root.
    r=0
    # to allow small noise, skip while r<n and ct[r] <= 10*tol
    while r < n and ct[r] <= 10*tol:
        r += 1
    if 0 < r < n and (r % 2 == 0):
        d=r//2
        if ct[:2*d].max() <= 10*tol:
            a=_root_d(ct,d,n,tol)
            if a is not None: outs.append(a)
    # also if r odd maybe force a zero? not.
    return outs
```
But if genuine d=0 with ct0 small but >tol? The forced d0 uses original ct0 (maybe small). If ct0 extremely small, a0 tiny causing huge coefficients and neg. The delayed d may skip if r=0 because ct0>tol? If ct0=1e-16, tol=1e-14, r increments. Good.
If ct0=0.001 (not small) d0. If ct0=0 due p0=0 and ct2>0, r loops: ct0<=tol r1; ct1 likely 0 <=tol r2; ct2>tol r=2 even. Good.

`_root_d(ct,d,n,tol)`:
```
b_len=n-d
start=2*d
if start + b_len > len(ct): return None
D=ct[start:start+b_len].copy()
# If D[0] small, force eps if start=0? For delayed, if D0 small due noise, force.
b0=math.sqrt(max(D[0], 1e-18*max(float(T?),1)))  # need T parameter. Pass T or maxD.
if b0 <= 0: return None
b=np.zeros(b_len); b[0]=b0
neg_abs=0; sumD = D.sum()
for k in range(1,b_len):
   pair = float(np.dot(b[1:k], b[k-1:0:-1])) if k>1 else 0.0
   val=(D[k]-pair)/(2*b0)
   if val<0: neg_abs += -val
   b[k]=max(0.0,val)
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 25.0  # headroom; if budget small, ensure > t0+1
    if deadline < t0+5: deadline=t0+budget*0.8
    import numpy as np etc already
    best_p=None; best_sc=math.inf
    def track(p):
       nonlocal best_p,best_sc
       p=_normalize(p)
       if p.size==0: return
       try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
       except Exception: return
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
           best_score=...; best_p=p.copy()
    # fallback
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)) or np.sum(np.clip(parent,0,None))<0.01:
       parent = np.ones(64)
    track(parent)
    # small guaranteed fallback if best none
    if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(32)/32; track(best_p)
    # If parent score already below target? Still improve.
    t_now=time.monotonic()
    # allocation: parent long
    parent_p = _normalize(parent)
    n_par = len(parent_p)
    # If parent length maybe too large? Always okay.
    # E1: improve parent length if enough time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        stop_parent = min(deadline - 60, time.monotonic() + 0.60*budget)  # maybe 780s
        cand = _improve(parent_p, stop_parent, seed+1)
        track(cand)
    # E2: other lengths
    leftover_stop = deadline - 40
    if time.monotonic() < leftover_stop:
        other_lengths = [96,128,160,192,256,320,448,512]
        # include parent n? maybe skip
        other_lengths = [L for L in other_lengths if L != n_par]
        # maybe add n_par*2 if <=1024, n_par//2
        for factor in (0.5, 1.5, 2.0):
            L=int(n_par*factor)
            if 32 <= L <= 1024: other_lengths.append(L)
        other_lengths = sorted(set(other_lengths))
        # allocate based on remaining time and number, but limit total 0.30 budget
        other_budget = min(0.30*budget, max(0, leftover_stop - time.monotonic()))
        per = other_budget / max(1,len(other_lengths))
        per = max(5.0, min(60.0, per))  # 5-60s
        for L in other_lengths:
            if time.monotonic() >= leftover_stop - 2 or budget? break
            stop = min(leftover_stop - 2, time.monotonic() + per)
            # candidate init: interp parent; if L==n_par use best_p? best_p may length n_par.
            if best_p is not None and best_p.size == L:
                init = _normalize(best_p)
            else:
                x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_par); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,L)
                init=np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent_p)
                # add tiny
                init = init + 1e-8
            # maybe for L in (128,192,256) also uniform quick? If time
            cand=_improve(init, stop, seed+1000+L)
            track(cand)
            # If time remains and L<=256, try uniform root
            if time.monotonic() + 10 < leftover_stop and L <= 256:
                init2=np.ones(L)/L
                cand2=_improve(init2, min(leftover_stop-2, time.monotonic()+0.5*per), seed+2000+L)
                track(cand2)
    # Final polish global best
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15:
        stop_final=deadline-15
        final_p = _improve(best_p, stop_final, seed+9999)  # but this may use full remaining with slices; okay
        track(final_p)
        # Additionally quick coord/transfer if still time
    # Ensure best_p valid; if not use uniform
    if best_p is None:
       return [1.0]
    # final exact validation
    try:
       sc=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))
    except Exception:
       sc=math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
       # fallback uniform 32
       dup=np.ones(32)/32
       try: sc2=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in dup]))
       except: sc2=math.inf
       if math.isfinite(sc2): return [float(x) for x in dup]
       return [1.0]
    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```

```
def _lp_improve(p, stop, seed=0, max_calls=40):
    p=_normalize(p); n=len(p); L=2*n-1

          no_imp+=2
          if no_imp>6: break
          continue
       # line search
       best_lambda=0.0; best_obj_current=current_sc
       for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0,0.15,0.85):

           q=_normalize(q)

              best_obj_current=sc; best_lambda=lam

       if best_lambda>0:
           p=_normalize((1-best_lambda)*p+best_g? Need save g for best_lambda. We can recompute or store q best.
```

Now, let's check mathematical correctness of gradient `np.correlate(w,p,'valid')`. Suppose n=2, w length3 [w0,w1,w2], p=[p0,p1]. np.correlate(w,p,'valid') returns length1? len(w)-len(p)+1=2. Wait 3-2+1=2. Good for i=0: w[0]*p[0]+w[1]*p[1] = ∑_j w[j] p[j]? Our formula grad_i=2∑_j p_j w_{i+j}. For i=0: w0 p0 + w1 p1. For i=1: w1 p0 + w2 p1. Numpy valid: output[0]=w[0]*p[0]+w[1]*p[1]; output[1]=w[1]*p[0]+w[2]*p[1]. Yes. Good.

But gradient of surrogate F=(1/β)log∑e^{β c_k} wrt p_i is 2∑_j p_j w_{i+j}. Yes. Since derivative of c_k wrt p_i: 2 p_{k-i} if k-i valid; summing over k with w_k -> 2∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i}. Let j=k-i => j=0..n-1 => w_{i+j}p_j. Correct. If p not normalized? Still gradient of c wrt p. Projection preserves sum1. Good.

Projection: Standard simplex projection onto sum=1, nonneg. If v has inf? handle.

```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    x=np.maximum(np.asarray(v,float), -np.inf)
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u*ind > (cssv-1.0)
    rho = np.max(np.where(cond, ind, 0)) - 1? Standard:
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    q=np.maximum(x-theta,0)
    # normalize due numeric
    s=q.sum(); if s>0: q=q/s
    return q
```
Check condition: For max q such that sum q=1, find rho largest satisfying (x_{(i)} - (sum_{j≤i} x_j)/i -1)≥0? Standard code:
```
x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
tot = x.cumsum()
cond = (x - (tot-1)/np.arange(1,n+1)) > 0
r = cond.nonzero()[0][-1]
theta = (tot[r]-1)/(r+1)
```
Use that.

Root recurrence dot: For b length up to 384, in Python loop `np.dot(b[1:k], b[k-1:0:-1])` alloc slices; fine. Could optimize by using `np.dot(b[1:k], b[1:k][::-1])`? Let's detail `_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
       return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
       p = np.ones_like(p)
       s = float(p.sum())
    p = p / s
    if p.size > 0 and p.max() > 1000.0:
       p = p / p.max() * 1000.0
       p = p / p.sum()
    return p
```
After normalize sum1, max≤1 (unless all zeros then ones). Good.

`_proj_simplex`:
```
def _proj_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -np.inf)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    n=u.size
    if n==0: return v
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = (u - (cssv - 1.0)/ind) > 0.0
    idx=cond.nonzero()[0]
    if idx.size==0:
       q=np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    else:
       r=idx[-1]
       theta=(cssv[r]-1.0)/(r+1.0)
       q=np.maximum(v-theta, 0.0)
       s=q.sum()
       if s<=0: q=np.full(n,1.0/n)
       else: q=q/s
    return q
```
Check condition: If v all equal, cond true? fine.

`_conv` with n<=512 direct:
```
def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    if n <= 512:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       N=1
       while N < L: N <<= 1
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:L]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
```
If a has length 0? not.

`_score_arr(p)` maybe:
```
def _score_arr(p):
   s=float(p.sum()); n=len(p)
   if s<1e-300: return math.inf
   c=_conv(p)
   m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
But most methods maintain sum1.

Root functions:
```
def _root_d(ct, d, n, scale):
    b_len = n - d
    if b_len <=0: return None
    start = 2*d
    if start + b_len > ct.size:
       return None
    D = np.asarray(ct[start:start+b_len], dtype=float)
    eps = max(1e-20 * max(float(scale), 1e-300), np.finfo(float).tiny)
    D0 = float(D[0])
    if D0 < eps:
       # if D0 too small, lift; but if exact d wrong, root may bad
       b0 = math.sqrt(eps)
    else:
       b0 = math.sqrt(max(D0, eps))
    b=np.zeros(b_len)
    b[0]=b0
    sumD = float(D.sum())
    neg_abs=0.0
    for k in range(1,b_len):
       if k==1: pair=0.0
       else: pair=float(np.dot(b[1:k], b[k-1:0:-1]))
       val=(float(D[k]) - pair) / (2.0*b0)
       if val < 0.0:
          neg_abs += -val
          val = 0.0
       b[k]=val
    # quality
    qual = neg_abs / max(sumD, 1e-30)
    if qual > 1.0:
       return None
    a=np.zeros(n)
    a[d:]=b
    return a, qual
```
Wait `D` length b_len (first half). For b_len=1, loop none. D.sum maybe c0. For correct root with p0 maybe D0 small? If d=0 and p0 very small but >0, b0 small; recurrence may huge. Quality may be large; reject. If delayed correct, D0 not too small? okay.

But if D0 forced eps when true 0 but no root, quality likely >1 reject. Good.

```
def _root_candidates(c,T,n):
    if T <= 0: return []
    ct=np.minimum(np.asarray(c,float), float(T))
    ct=0.5*(ct+ct[::-1])
    scale=max(float(T), float(np.max(ct)), 1e-30)
    tol=1e-12*scale
    res=[]
    # d0 with D0 maybe original ct0; if ct0==0, skip forced? Could still try if no delayed
    if n>0:
       # d=0
       # if ct[0] > 0 up to maybe; if ct[0] too tiny, lifting can yield invalid; try only if ct[0] > tol*0.1 or n small?
       if ct[0] > tol*1e-3 or n <= 16:
          a,q=_root_d(ct,0,n,scale)
          if a is not None: res.append((a,q))
       # delayed
       r=0
       while r < n and ct[r] <= 10*tol:
           r += 1
       if 0 < r < n and (r % 2 == 0) and float(ct[:max(1,r)].max()) <= 10*tol:
          d=r//2
          a,q=_root_d(ct,d,n,scale)
          if a is not None: res.append((a,q))
       # also try forced d0 with eps if no res or ct0 small maybe
       if not res or ct[0] <= tol:
          ct_force=ct.copy()
          ct_force[0]=max(ct_force[0], max(tol, 1e-18*scale))
          a,q=_root_d(ct_force,0,n,scale)
          if a is not None and q < 0.5: res.append((a,q))
    # Deduplicate? We'll just return
    return res
```

```
def _coord_polish(p, stop, seed=0):
```

```
def _gradient_soft(p, stop, seed=0):
    p=_normalize(p); n=len(p); S=1
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=2*n*float(_conv(p).max())
    rng=... not used
    betas=(20,60,150,400)
    max_noimp=200
    iters_total=0
    for beta in betas:
        noimp=0
        for it in range(5000):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1: break
            c=_conv(p); M=float(c.max())
            sc=2*n*M
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-13: best...
            if noimp > max_noimp: break
            z=beta*(c - M)
            z=np.where(z < -40.0, -40.0, z)
            w=np.exp(z); sw=float(w.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(sw) or sw <=0:
               active=np.argsort(c)[-max(1,n//10):]; w[active]=1; sw=w.sum()
            w/=sw
            grad=2.0*np.correlate(w,p,mode='valid')
            # grad length n? Ensure
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if gn <= 1e-300: break
            mean=1.0/n
            base = mean / gn
            best_q=None; best_obj=sc
            for coeff in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0):
                q = p - base*coeff*grad
                q = _proj_simplex(q)
                q = _normalize(q)
                obj=2*n*float(_conv(q).max())
                if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
                    best_obj=obj; best_q=q
            if best_q is not None:
                p=best_q; noimp=0
            else:
                noimp +=1
    return _normalize(best)
```
If beta high, z clipped to -40, weights for lags more than M-40/beta zero; active maybe many. Good.

Step base = mean/gn. If gn large, steps small. Could also divide by n? mean already 1/n. Good.

Now, `_lp_improve` line search with current p normalized. Need store best_q.
```
def _lp_improve(p, stop, seed=0):
    if linprog is None: return p
    p=_normalize(p); n=len(p)
    if n > 800: return p
    c=_conv(p); sc=2*n*float(c.max()); best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
    noimp=0; calls=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 5 and calls < 80:
       c=_conv(p); M=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*M
       if sc < best_sc -1e-13: best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc
       g=_lp_direction(p, M*(1+1e-10))
       calls +=1
       if g is None:
          noimp +=2
          if noimp>8: break
          continue
       if float(np.linalg.norm(g-p)) < 1e-10:
          noimp +=1
          if noimp>8: break
          continue
       best_q=None; best_obj=sc
       for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0,0.15,0.85):
          if time.monotonic() > stop-1: break
          q=(1-lam)*p + lam*g
          q=_normalize(q)
          obj=2*n*float(_conv(q).max())
          if obj < best_obj -1e-13:
             best_obj=obj; best_q=q
       if best_q is not None:
          p=best_q; noimp=0
       else:
          noimp +=1
          if noimp>8: break
    return _normalize(best)
```
If LP slow, calls small. Max 80 too many? slice small. Time bound. Maybe max_calls=50.

`_lp_direction`:
```
def _lp_direction(f,M):
    n=len(f); L=2*n-1
    if M <= 0: return None
    A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i+n, i] = f
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=np.full(L, M)
    bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs',
                   options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None
    g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    s=float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
       # no gain; but maybe direction? return if significantly different? If s close1 and g different maybe could still improve? LP optimum sum=1; direction no.
       if s > 1e-12 and float(np.linalg.norm(g/s - f)) > 1e-8:
          return g/s
       return None
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    g/=s
    # clean small
    g[np.abs(g)<1e-15]=0.0
    return g if g.size and np.isfinite(g).all() else None
```
Actually if s=1 but g different, moving to g sum1 and constraints f*g≤M; could improve? The objective of LP sum no gain, but g could have lower f*f? Not sure. Return if dist> to allow line search? It might not improve; line search will test. But many LP calls with s=1 could waste. Maybe if s <=1+1e-7 return None. We'll do return None unless norm(g_p - f)>1e-6 and s<=1? Could try. But to save, if s <=1+1e-9 return None. Since parent claim s≥1. If no sum gain, likely no useful direction. Use `if s <= 1.0 + 1e-10: return None`.

Now, `_improve` method. Need to avoid calling methods on p length 1. Use `if n<2: return p`.

```
def _improve(p, stop, seed=0):
    p=_normalize(p); n=len(p)
    if n<2 or time.monotonic() >= stop: return p
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    alt=max(1.0, stop - t_start)
    slices = [('root',0.08), ('coord',0.38), ('transfer',0.27), ('grad',0.08), ('root2',0.04), ('lp',0.08), ('coord2',0.07)]
    for name, frac in slices:
       if time.monotonic() >= stop - 0.5: break
       method_stop = min(stop - 0.5, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, frac*alt))
       if method_stop <= time.monotonic(): continue
       try:
          if name in ('root','root2'): p=_root_improve(p, method_stop, seed+1)
          elif name=='coord': p=_coord_polish(p, method_stop, seed+2)
          elif name=='transfer': p=_transfer_polish(p, method_stop, seed+3)
          elif name=='grad': p=_gradient_soft(p, method_stop, seed+4)
          elif name=='lp': p=_lp_improve(p, method_stop, seed+5)
       except Exception:
          pass
    return _normalize(p)
```
If root slice 0.08 and alt 800 =>64s. Good.

Potential cumulative time: If each method gets full slice from current time and all use full, total roughly sum fracs*alt? Since start time advances, method k stop = start_k + frac*alt. If previous used full, start_k = t_start + sum_prev fracs*alt (plus early exact). Then stop for k = t_start + sum_prev + frac*alt = t_start + sum_up_to_k*alt. So total full use = sum fracs*alt = alt. Good. If previous early, next stop = now + frac*alt, which is earlier than original planned? Actually if previous early by Δ, next start earlier, stop earlier by Δ relative to planned, preserving total. Good.

```python
import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

_TARGET_SCORE = 1.50274349

_FACTORS = (
    0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50,
    0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00, 1.10, 1.25, 1.50, 1.75,
    2.00, 3.00, 5.00, 8.00
)
_MEAN_FACTORS = (0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00, 5.00, 10.00)
_TRANS_FRACS = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80, 0.95, 1.00)


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.sum())
    p = p / s
    return p


def _proj_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -np.inf)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u - (cssv - 1.0) / ind) > 0.0
    idx = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / (r + 1.0)
    q = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = q / s
    return q


def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        N = 1
        while N < L:
            N <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _score_normalized(p):
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    c = _conv(p)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _root_d(ct, d, n, scale):
    b_len = n - d
    if b_len <= 0:
        return None, math.inf
    start = 2 * d
    if start + b_len > ct.size:
        return None, math.inf
    D = np.asarray(ct[start:start + b_len], dtype=np.float64)
    if D.size != b_len:
        return None, math.inf
    sumD = float(D.sum())
    if sumD <= 1e-300:
        return None, math.inf
    eps = max(1e-20 * max(float(scale), 1e-300), np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    D0 = float(D[0])
    if not math.isfinite(D0) or D0 < eps:
        D0 = eps
    b0 = math.sqrt(D0)
    b = np.zeros(b_len, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = b0
    neg_abs = 0.0
    for k in range(1, b_len):
        if k == 1:
            pair = 0.0
        else:
            pair = float(np.dot(b[1:k], b[k - 1:0:-1]))
        val = (float(D[k]) - pair) / (2.0 * b0)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return None, math.inf
        if val < 0.0:
            neg_abs += -val
            val = 0.0
        b[k] = val
    qual = neg_abs / max(sumD, 1e-300)
    if qual > 1.0:
        return None, qual
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[d:] = b
    return a, qual


def _root_candidates(c, T, n):
    if T <= 0.0:
        return []
    ct = np.minimum(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64), float(T))
    ct = 0.5 * (ct + ct[::-1])
    scale = max(float(T), float(np.max(ct)), 1e-300)
    tol = 1e-12 * scale
    out = []

    if ct[0] > tol * 1e-3 or n <= 16:
        a, q = _root_d(ct, 0, n, scale)
        if a is not None:
            out.append((a, q))

    r = 0
    while r < n and ct[r] <= 10.0 * tol:
        r += 1
    if 0 < r < n and (r % 2 == 0):
        d = r // 2
        a, q = _root_d(ct, d, n, scale)
        if a is not None:
            out.append((a, q))

    if not out or ct[0] <= tol:
        cf = ct.copy()
        cf[0] = max(float(cf[0]), max(tol, 1e-18 * scale))
        a, q = _root_d(cf, 0, n, scale)
        if a is not None and q < 0.5:
            out.append((a, q))

    return out


def _root_improve(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return p
    c = _conv(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_normalized(best)
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return p

    for _round in range(40):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        targets = []
        for q in (0.999, 0.99, 0.98, 0.97, 0.95, 0.93, 0.90, 0.87,
                  0.84, 0.80, 0.75, 0.70, 0.65, 0.60, 0.55, 0.50,
                  0.45, 0.40, 0.30, 0.20):
            targets.append(q * M)
        avg = 1.0 / (2 * n - 1)
        for rr in (1.0, 1.10, 1.20, 1.30, 1.40, 1.50, 1.60):
            targets.append(rr * avg)
        targets.append(_TARGET_SCORE / (2.0 * n))
        try:
            pcts = np.percentile(c, [25, 50, 75, 90, 99])
            targets.extend([float(x) for x in pcts])
        except Exception:
            pass

        ts = []
        for t in targets:
            if t is None:
                continue
            t = float(t)
            if math.isfinite(t) and 0.0 < t <= M * (1.0 + 1e-9):
                ts.append(t)
        if not ts:
            break
        ts = sorted(set(ts), reverse=True)

        roots = []
        for T in ts:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.0:
                break
            roots.extend(_root_candidates(c, T, n))

        improved = False
        for a, q in roots:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.0:
                break
            aa = _normalize(a)
            cands = [aa]
            cands.append(0.35 * aa + 0.65 * best)
            cands.append(0.65 * aa + 0.35 * best)
            cands.append(0.85 * aa + 0.15 * best)
            for mix in cands:
                mm = _normalize(mix)
                sc = _score_normalized(mm)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = mm
                    best_sc = sc
                    c = _conv(best)
                    improved = True
                    break
        if not improved:
            break

    return _normalize(best)


def _coord_polish(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return p

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c = _conv(p)
    S = 1.0
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * cmax
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mean = 1.0 / float(n)

    def eval_b(b):
        b = float(b)
        da = b - a
        if not math.isfinite(da):
            return math.inf, 0.0
        S2 = S + da
        if S2 <= 1e-300:
            return math.inf, 0.0
        tmp = 2.0 * da * pjs
        tmp[i] += da * da
        m_aff = float(np.max(c_aff + tmp))
        M = m_aff if m_aff > nonmax else nonmax
        if M < 0.0:
            M = 0.0
        obj = 2.0 * n * M / (S2 * S2)
        return obj, M

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.0:
                break
            i = int(i)
            a = float(p[i])
            ks = np.arange(i, i + n, dtype=np.int64)
            pjs = p
            c_aff = c[ks]

            left_max = -1.0
            if i > 0:
                left_max = float(np.max(c[:i]))
            right_max = -1.0
            if i + n < L:
                right_max = float(np.max(c[i + n:]))
            nonmax = max(left_max, right_max)

            curr_sc = 2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S)

            bs = list(_FACTORS * a)
            if a < 10.0 * mean:
                bs.extend(_MEAN_FACTORS * mean)
            bs.append(a)
            bs = np.unique(np.clip(np.asarray(bs, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, None))

            best_b = a
            best_obj = curr_sc
            best_M = cmax

            for b in bs:
                b = float(b)
                if abs(b - a) <= 1e-15 * max(1.0, abs(a), mean):
                    continue
                obj, M = eval_b(b)
                if obj < best_obj:
                    best_obj = obj
                    best_b = b
                    best_M = M

            if best_obj < curr_sc - 1e-13 and abs(best_b - a) > 1e-15:
                if best_b > 0.0:
                    lo = 0.5 * best_b
                    hi = max(2.0 * best_b, 20.0 * mean, 15.0 * max(a, mean))
                else:
                    lo = 0.0
                    hi = max(20.0 * mean, 10.0 * mean)
                if hi <= lo:
                    hi = lo + max(1e-8, 10.0 * mean)
                for _ in range(14):
                    m1 = lo + (hi - lo) / 3.0
                    m2 = hi - (hi - lo) / 3.0
                    o1, M1 = eval_b(m1)
                    o2, M2 = eval_b(m2)
                    if o1 < o2:
                        hi = m2
                    else:
                        lo = m1
                    if hi - lo <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, mean):
                        break
                bm = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
                om, Mm = eval_b(bm)
                if om < best_obj:
                    best_obj = om
                    best_b = bm
                    best_M = Mm

            if best_obj < best_sc - 1e-12 and abs(best_b - a) > 1e-15:
                da = best_b - a
                S2 = S + da
                tmp = 2.0 * da * pjs
                tmp[i] += da * da
                c[ks] += tmp
                p[i] = best_b
                S = S2
                cmax = best_M
                if best_obj < best_sc:
                    best_sc = best_obj
                    best = _normalize(p)
                improved = True

        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
            c = _conv(p)
            S = 1.0
            cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            break
        p = p / s
        c = _conv(p)
        S = 1.0
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = 2.0 * n * cmax
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = p.copy()
        if not improved:
            break

    return _normalize(best)


def _transfer_search(i, j, p, c, S, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ks1 = np.arange(i, i + n, dtype=np.int64)
    ks2 = np.arange(j, j + n, dtype=np.int64)
    ks = np.unique(np.concatenate((ks1, ks2)))

    mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
    mask[ks] = True
    if np.any(~mask):
        non_max = float(np.max(c[~mask]))
    else:
        non_max = 0.0

    valid_i = (ks >= i) & (ks < i + n)
    valid_j = (ks >= j) & (ks < j + n)
    pi = np.where(valid_i, p[np.clip(ks - i, 0, n - 1)], 0.0)
    pj = np.where(valid_j, p[np.clip(ks - j, 0, n - 1)], 0.0)
    Acoef = pi - pj
    G = np.zeros(ks.size, dtype=np.float64)

    for kval in (2 * i, 2 * j):
        pos = int(np.searchsorted(ks, kval))
        if pos < ks.size and ks[pos] == kval:
            G[pos] += 1.0
    ksum = i + j
    pos = int(np.searchsorted(ks, ksum))
    if pos < ks.size and ks[pos] == ksum:
        G[pos] -= 2.0

    tvals = [0.0]
    if p[j] > 0.0:
        tvals.extend(fr * p[j] for fr in _TRANS_FRACS)
    if p[i] > 0.0:
        tvals.extend(-fr * p[i] for fr in _TRANS_FRACS)
    ts = np.unique(np.asarray(tvals, dtype=np.float64))

    a = float(p[i])
    b = float(p[j])

    def obj_t(t):
        t = float(t)
        delta = t * Acoef + (t * t) * G
        m_aff = float(np.max(c[ks] + delta))
        M = m_aff if m_aff > non_max else non_max
        if M < 0.0:
            M = 0.0
        return 2.0 * n * M / (S * S), M

    best_t = 0.0
    best_sc, best_M = obj_t(0.0)
    best_delta = np.zeros(ks.size, dtype=np.float64)

    for t in ts:
        if abs(t) <= 1e-18:
            continue
        sc, M = obj_t(t)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best_t = float(t)
            best_M = M
            best_delta = (best_t * Acoef + best_t * best_t * G).astype(np.float64)

    if best_sc < obj_t(0.0)[0] - 1e-13 and abs(best_t) > 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmin(np.abs(ts - best_t)))
        t_min = -a
        t_max = b
        if idx <= 0:
            lo = best_t
            hi = min(t_max, best_t + max(1e-8, 0.25 * max(a, b)))
        elif idx >= ts.size - 1:
            hi = best_t
            lo = max(t_min, best_t - max(1e-8, 0.25 * max(a, b)))
        else:
            lo = max(t_min, ts[idx - 1])
            hi = min(t_max, ts[idx + 1])
        if hi > lo and hi < t_max:
            hi = min(t_max, hi)
        if lo < t_min:
            lo = t_min
        if hi > lo:
            for _ in range(14):
                m1 = lo + (hi - lo) / 3.0
                m2 = hi - (hi - lo) / 3.0
                o1, M1 = obj_t(m1)
                o2, M2 = obj_t(m2)
                if o1 < o2:
                    hi = m2
                else:
                    lo = m1
                if hi - lo <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(best_t), mean if False else 1e-3):
                    break
            tm = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            om, Mm = obj_t(tm)
            if om < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = om
                best_t = float(tm)
                best_M = Mm
                best_delta = (best_t * Acoef + best_t * best_t * G).astype(np.float64)

    return best_t, best_sc, best_delta, best_M, ks


def _transfer_polish(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return p

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c = _conv(p)
    S = float(p.sum())
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    cur_sc = 2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc
    stall = 0
    accepts = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 1.0 and it < 100000:
        it += 1

        if accepts and accepts % 500 == 0:
            p = _normalize(p)
            c = _conv(p)
            S = 1.0
            cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            cur_sc = 2.0 * n * cmax

        if it % 100 == 1:
            thresh = cmax - 0.05 * cmax
            active = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
            if active.size == 0:
                active = np.where(c > 0.0)[0]
            if active.size == 0:
                active = np.array([0], dtype=int)

        used_active = False
        if rng.random() < 0.80 and active.size:
            k = int(active[int(rng.integers(active.size))])
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(k, n - 1)
            if hi > lo:
                i = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
                j = k - i
                if i != j:
                    used_active = True

        if not used_active:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j += 1

        t, sc, delta, M, ks = _transfer_search(i, j, p, c, S, n)
        if sc < cur_sc - 1e-13 and abs(t) > 1e-15:
            p[i] += t
            p[j] -= t
            c[ks] += delta
            p[np.abs(p) < 1e-300] = 0.0
            cur_sc = sc
            cmax = M
            accepts += 1
            stall = 0
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = sc
                best = p.copy()
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 800:
                p = _normalize(best)
                c = _conv(p)
                S = 1.0
                cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                cur_sc = 2.0 * n * cmax
                stall = 0

    return _normalize(best)


def _gradient_soft(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return p

    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_normalized(best)
    mean = 1.0 / float(n)

    for beta in (15, 40, 100, 250):
        noimp = 0
        for _it in range(6000):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 1.0:
                break
            c = _conv(p)
            M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            sc = 2.0 * n * M
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = sc
                best = p.copy()

            if noimp > 250:
                break

            z = beta * (c - M)
            z = np.where(z < -40.0, -40.0, z)
            w = np.exp(z)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                active = np.argsort(c)[-max(1, n // 10):]
                w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
                w[active] = 1.0
                sw = float(w.sum())
            w = w / sw

            grad = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, p, mode="valid")
            if grad.size != n:
                grad = grad[:n]
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if gn <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(gn):
                break

            base = mean / gn
            found = False
            for coeff in (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
                q = p - base * coeff * grad
                q = _proj_simplex(q)
                q = _normalize(q)
                obj = _score_normalized(q)
                if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < sc - 1e-13:
                    p = q
                    sc = obj
                    found = True
                    break
            noimp = 0 if found else noimp + 1

    return _normalize(best)


def _lp_direction(f, M):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 0 or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i + n, i] = f
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(L, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res.status != 0 or res.x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1.0 + 1e-10:
        return None
    g = g / s
    g[np.abs(g) < 1e-15] = 0.0
    return g


def _lp_improve(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if linprog is None or n < 2 or n > 800:
        return p

    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_normalized(best)
    noimp = 0
    calls = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 8.0 and calls < 80:
        c = _conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = sc
            best = p.copy()

        g = _lp_direction(p, M * (1.0 + 1e-9))
        calls += 1
        if g is None:
            noimp += 2
            if noimp > 8:
                break
            continue
        if float(np.linalg.norm(g - p)) < 1e-10:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 8:
                break
            continue

        found = False
        for lam in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90, 1.00, 0.15, 0.85):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 2.0:
                break
            q = (1.0 - lam) * p + lam * g
            q = _normalize(q)
            obj = _score_normalized(q)
            if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < sc - 1e-13:
                p = q
                sc = obj
                found = True
                break

        if found:
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 8:
                break

    return _normalize(best)


def _improve(p, stop, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    alt = max(1.0, stop - t_start)
    slices = (
        ("root", 0.08),
        ("coord", 0.38),
        ("transfer", 0.27),
        ("grad", 0.08),
        ("root2", 0.04),
        ("lp", 0.08),
        ("coord2", 0.07),
    )

    for name, frac in slices:
        if time.monotonic() >= stop - 0.5:
            break
        method_stop = min(stop - 0.5, time.monotonic() + max(1.0, frac * alt))
        if method_stop <= time.monotonic() + 0.05:
            continue
        try:
            if name == "root" or name == "root2":
                p = _root_improve(p, method_stop, seed + 1)
            elif name == "coord" or name == "coord2":
                p = _coord_polish(p, method_stop, seed + 2)
            elif name == "transfer":
                p = _transfer_polish(p, method_stop, seed + 3)
            elif name == "grad":
                p = _gradient_soft(p, method_stop, seed + 4)
            elif name == "lp":
                p = _lp_improve(p, method_stop, seed + 5)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return _normalize(p)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 30.0
    if deadline < t0 + 10.0:
        deadline = t0 + 0.8 * budget

    best_p = None
    best_score = math.inf

    def track(p):
        nonlocal best_p, best_score
        if p is None:
            return
        p = _normalize(p)
        if p.size == 0:
            return
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is None or parent.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
        parent = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    track(parent)
    if best_p is None:
        track(np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0)

    parent_p = _normalize(parent)
    n_par = parent_p.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 12345)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        stop_parent = min(deadline - 45.0, t0 + 0.62 * budget)
        try:
            cand = _improve(parent_p, stop_parent, seed + 1)
            track(cand)
        except Exception:
            pass

    other_stop = deadline - 30.0
    other_budget = min(0.28 * budget, max(0.0, other_stop - time.monotonic()))
    if time.monotonic() < other_stop - 5.0 and other_budget > 20.0:
        base_n = max(int(n_par), 8)
        near = []
        for f in (0.50, 0.667, 0.75, 0.85, 1.00, 1.15, 1.25, 1.50, 2.00):
            L = int(round(base_n * f))
            if 4 <= L <= 1024 and L != n_par:
                near.append(L)
        small_medium_large = [
            4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10,
            12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64,
            80, 96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 320,
            448, 512, 640, 768
        ]
        ordered = []
        for L in near + small_medium_large:
            if 4 <= L <= 1024 and L != n_par and L not in ordered:
                ordered.append(L)
        ordered = ordered[:24]

        if ordered:
            per = other_budget / float(len(ordered))
            per = max(8.0, min(90.0, per))
            x_base = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent_p.size)

            for li, L in enumerate(ordered):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= other_stop - 4.0:
                    break
                stop_here = min(other_stop - 4.0, now + per)

                base = best_p if (best_p is not None and best_p.size > 0) else parent_p
                if base.size == 1:
                    init_interp = np.full(L, float(base[0]), dtype=np.float64)
                else:
                    init_interp = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L), np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, base.size), base)

                if L <= 32:
                    ii = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
                    inits = [
                        np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L,
                        init_interp.copy(),
                        np.linspace(1.0, L, L),
                        np.maximum(0.2 + 0.8 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * ii / max(1, L))), 1e-6),
                        np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, L)) + 0.1,
                    ]
                    k = min(5, len(inits))
                    slice_t = max(1.0, per / float(k))
                    for j, ini in enumerate(inits[:k]):
                        if time.monotonic() >= other_stop - 4.0:
                            break
                        s_stop = min(other_stop - 4.0, time.monotonic() + slice_t)
                        if s_stop <= time.monotonic():
                            continue
                        try:
                            cand = _improve(ini, s_stop, seed + 10000 + 100 * L + j)
                            track(cand)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                else:
                    try:
                        cand = _improve(init_interp, stop_here, seed + 10000 + L)
                        track(cand)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    if L <= 96 and time.monotonic() + 5.0 < other_stop:
                        try:
                            cand2 = _improve(np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L, min(other_stop - 4.0, time.monotonic() + 0.5 * per),
                                             seed + 20000 + L)
                            track(cand2)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        stop_final = deadline - 12.0
        try:
            final_p = _improve(best_p, stop_final, seed + 99991)
            track(final_p)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        try:
            q = _coord_polish(best_p, deadline - 6.0, seed + 424242)
            track(q)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_p is None:
        fallback = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        try:
            evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fallback])
        except Exception:
            fallback = [1.0]
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    try:
        final_check = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))
    except Exception:
        final_check = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(final_check):
        fallback = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        try:
            if math.isfinite(float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fallback]))):
                return [float(x) for x in fallback]
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
